# French second-batch fan-out (✅ COMPLETE at #115, closed 2026-07-10)

**CLOSE (operator ruling 2026-07-10, via AskUserQuestion):** the fan-out is **COMPLETE at #115** (175 fr activities, wrapper 9.231). The 2 activities previously listed as "remaining rebuildable grammar L.*" are **DEFERRED as design-not-fan** — on deeper read both target **English/Germanic morphology French structurally lacks**, joining the 8 English-phonics-locked as deferred (each would need a NEW French-specific activity, not a rebuild of the English engine):
- **hattie-whose-is-it (L.1.1.b possessive)** — mechanic = pick the correctly-marked possessive (dog's / dogs / dogs'; de fanned via German Genitiv-s *Annas*). **French has NO possessive morpheme** — possession = « l'os du chien » / « le ballon d'Anna », a `de`-construction with the marker in the MIDDLE, not a prefix on the owner (the owner-word-then-thing card layout is English word-order). A French version = a NEW activity (déterminants possessifs mon/ma/mes · son/sa/ses, or the de-complement du nom).
- **compound-meaning / skips-word-welding-yard (L.2.4.d)** — engine welds **two picturable nouns** where the whole is *a-kind-of-the-head* (fishbowl = a bowl FOR fish; `weldedHTML` head-tinting + the head/supply/weld/ticket/predict cogs all assume this; `composeMeaning`='a '+head+RELATION+glossMod is English-locked; the de fan grades via `cmIsAnswer` on the stored `kind` tag). French's transparent N-N compounds of that exact shape are **few** (chou-fleur / oiseau-mouche / porte-fenêtre / arc-en-ciel / bonhomme de neige ≈5, several with a middle preposition → break the two-block weld); France's *productive* compounds are verb+noun (porte-manteau / grille-pain / tire-bouchon / taille-crayon) — but a *taille-crayon* is NOT a kind of crayon → the head/supply/weld cogs break. A rich French rebuild needs a NEW mots-composés engine, not a 0-core-line fan.

**✅ DEPLOYED LIVE 2026-07-10** (release `Gm6dzuTWGYtUB305o-9g4`). The batched deploy = exactly the **115 fr commits** `54f6f319..fe46c22d` (clean fast-forward; the German second-batch + SEO work were already ancestors/live — NOT part of this deploy). Chain: `git pull --ff-only` → `cp "mini tools/"*-activity.js *-activities.json *-activity.html /var/www/lcs-media/mini-tools/` (the nginx-served copy; `activities.ts` reads manifests at BUILD time via the `public/mini-tools`→served symlink, so slug.fr must land BEFORE the build — §20.4; deploy.sh does NOT sync mini-tools) → `bash deploy.sh` (next-start model, ~3min). **Verified:** all sampled `/fr/activities/<slug>` routes 200 (301→https→200; curl `-L`) across engines+grades incl. an early-fan (pips-round) + latest (tempo #115); de/en unregressed, no fr leak; health green.

**⚠⚠ LATENT BUILD BLOCKER the deploy exposed — fixed `b8149240` (dedup):** the fan-out repeatedly INSERTED a separate `{fr:…}` entry near the top of GRADE_OVERRIDE/STRAND_OVERRIDE in `activities/[slug]/page.tsx` instead of MERGING into the existing `{de:…}` lower down → **6 duplicate object keys** (GRADE: wake-up-pip, opposites; STRAND: field-guide, bea-two-bookshelves, nila-pond, opposites) = a TypeScript "object literal cannot have multiple properties with the same name" error. It stayed latent through all 115 activities because the per-activity DoD ran `node --check` on the activity JS but **NEVER `tsc` on page.tsx** (no deploy during the fan-out) and the RENDER assertion only regex-checked the single new entry. The first batched-deploy build caught it (page.tsx:44). Fix = a deterministic node dedup (merge each pair's de+fr into one entry, behaviour-preserving — `effGrade`/`effStrand` read `[row.id][locale]`). **FORWARD RULE (now in the recipe): after ANY page.tsx GRADE/STRAND edit, run `cd frontend && npx tsc --noEmit` OR a per-map dup-key scan (partition by the GRADE_OVERRIDE / STRAND_OVERRIDE line ranges — a key legitimately appears once in EACH map, so only 2+ within one map is a real dup). `node --check` cannot catch TS dup keys.**

**What:** the parallel-to-German program — fan the **122 EN-only lcs-shell second-batch activities** out to **French** (locale `fr`), ONE per plan-mode session, via a **3-agent native French ensemble** (CP pedagogue + linguist + B2C/SEO content creator), **rebuild not translate** for the French **programmes officiels** (Éducation nationale), cycle 2 (CP/CE1/CE2). Sibling of [[project-german-secondbatch-fanout]] — same recipe, French curriculum/language.

**DEPLOY POLICY (operator-locked 2026-07-06):** build ALL French activities LOCALLY, **commit + push only, NO deploy per activity**; deploy the whole French set in ONE batch at the very end (unlike German #1, the first French activity was NOT deployed live). Per-activity commits accumulate on `pivot/printable-business-toolkit`.

**Operator cadence:** operator says **"continue"** (or "switch to plan mode + continue") → EnterPlanMode → 3-agent French ensemble (content into the plan file) → ExitPlanMode approval → build → full LOCAL DoD (§A.13.62) → show operator the local screenshots → operator approves → commit+push (NO deploy). ONE at a time.

**Locked rulings (whole program):**
1. **rebuild, not translate** — genuinely sound for a French CP classroom; native 3-agent ensemble per activity.
2. **NEVER skip** (same as German) — find the French teaching point for EVERY activity incl. English-specific literacy.
3. Build order mirrors German: math/measurement/geometry first, literacy rebuilt when reached.
4. **Character names KEPT** across locales (Tilly, Inchie, …). In-app title vs SEO page_title may differ (both keep the name).

**Infra (French is a full locale — NO scaffolding needed):** `frontend/messages/activity-content/fr.json` prose exists; `scripts/verify-activity-content-fr.js` (French §20.10 gate: rejects "Common Core"/CCSS-code, unique about[0] opener, no {placeholder}, floor ids≥50); `visual-qa-activity.js --locale=fr`; route already wires `EDUCATIONAL_FRAMEWORK_BY_LOCALE.fr='Programmes officiels'`, `strand-names.ts` fr for all math strands (M&D→« Grandeurs et mesures », Geometry→« Espace et géométrie », etc.), `seo.educational_level` fr: preschool=Maternelle / kindergarten=Grande section / **grade_1=CP** / grade_2=CE1 / grade_3=CE2 / grade_4=CM1. `GRADE_OVERRIDE`/`STRAND_OVERRIDE` maps are **de-only** — add an `fr` entry ONLY when the French grade/domain differs from the CCSS auto-map (none needed for the length activities: CP + Grandeurs et mesures resolve by default).

**Per-activity recipe (activity-layer ONLY; `<engine>-core.js` / `lcs-shell.*` / Direction-A CSS = 0 lines, `git diff --name-only` proof):**
- `mini tools/<engine>-activity.js`: add `fr` to the string maps (`COLOR_L`/`L`/`strings`), generalize any locale-hardcoded helper to read `[LANG]` (e.g. lay-units NUMW), keep `{n}/{a}/{b}` placeholders; French « ! »/« ? » spacing (space before). If a longer French prompt overflows a narrow viewport, tighten the ACTIVITY's own injected CSS at a `@media (max-width:340px)` breakpoint (activity-layer, allowed) — never the shell; re-verify EN+DE unchanged.
- `mini tools/<engine>-activities.json`: add `fr` to `slug`/`page_title`/`page_intro` (+ `promptL10n.fr` per round if present). Slug ASCII-folded, cite the CP domain, **NO "Common Core"/CCSS code**; collision-check vs existing fr slugs.
- `frontend/messages/activity-content/fr.json`: add `prose[<id>]` full-override (about≥2/practices≥3/howToPlay≥2/learningGoals≥2; unique opener; no code/Common-Core; straight ASCII apostrophes, guillemets « » only around quoted terms).
- `mini tools/<engine>-activity.html`: bump `<engine>-activity.js?v=N`.
- `frontend/app/[locale]/activities/[slug]/page.tsx`: bump `ACTIVITY_WRAPPER_VERSION` (now at **9.212** after #96).

**Gates per build (lean):** `node --check` + JSON-valid + `verify-activity-content-fr.js` + `preflight-activity-routes.js` + `visual-qa --activity=<id> --locale=fr` (320·360·412·768·1024·1366 × every round) + EN-regression `--locale=en` (+ DE if the change touched shared activity CSS) + personal Read 360/768/1024 + `git diff --name-only` 0-core. NO whole-catalog sweeps.

## ⚠ POOL CORRECTION (operator 2026-07-09) — NOT exhausted; target ~200 FR activities; cadence = AUTONOMOUS MATH-FIRST
The prior "light-fan pool EXHAUSTED" (after #65) was WRONG — it only covered the RL/RI/L literacy sub-pool. **Full manifest enum: 205 activity coords, ~150 with a FR fan (through #96), ~55 without** (reliable signal = `slug.fr` absent; ⚠ NOT the `prose` count — fr.json prose lives under `fr.prose.<id>`, so an Object.keys(fr) top-level check gives 3, not 141). The bulk of the gap = **clean de-fanned (en+de) MATH/measurement/geometry** activities (German-fanned → proven fannable); **~18 clean fannable MATH remain** (Grade-3 tail; path to ~200); ~15 = English-phoneme/morphology → DEFER (need protected-core changes). **Operator chose cadence = "Autonomous, math-first"**: I pick the order (cleanest de-fanned math first, then fannable literacy), ONE per plan-mode session with full ensemble + §A.13.62 DoD, surface only genuine forks; each "continue" = build the next. **DEFER (need core changes):** English-phoneme (cvc-builder×3, pepper, sound-boxes, stretch-giraffe, word-clinic, choice-board.read-cvc-word + onset-rime-blend, domino-two-part, rhyme-shop, mending-basket) + English-morphology (daisy/hattie/compound-meaning/tempo) + booker (accent-collation) + storybook (PixiJS); set-aside near-dups linc/marina.

113. **daisy-plate-stack `plurals.l-k-1-c`** → « Marguerite la cane : le pluriel des noms » / slug `le-pluriel-des-noms-marque-s-et-x-ce1` — commit `f9ed85e9`. **7th literacy fan — the FIRST grammar L.* fan.** **CE1** (L.K.1.c, regular plural nouns). **le pluriel des noms.** Marguerite la cane 🦆 sert les commandes ; l'enfant lit une phrase (« Je voudrais trois ___. ») + touche le mot AU PLURIEL bien écrit parmi 3. 10 rounds. Core = **`plural-noun-core.js`** (`PluralNounCore`; derives the EN +s/+es rule). **`plural-noun-core.js`/`lcs-shell.*`/`strand-names.ts` = 0 lines.** ⚠⚠ **EXPLICIT-ROUNDS FAN (rule-derived core):** the EN core derives the answer by the +s/+es rule, which DOESN'T hold in French → fr carries **EXPLICIT `roundsL10n.fr` `{id, band, sentence, chips, answer}`** (like de, whose comment says "German plural can't be rule-derived") ; the activity's `setupTask`/`isCorrect` already branch on `round.chips`/`round.answer` for de → I extended the arm to `(LANG === 'de' || LANG === 'fr')`. **⚠⚠ FRENCH PLURAL = a WRITTEN-ORTHOGRAPHY skill (the -s is SILENT: chat/chats sound identical → the child must SEE it):** featured the regular **-s** (chat→chats, fleur→fleurs, table→tables, pomme→pommes, fourchette→fourchettes, livre→livres) + the **-eau/-eu→-x rule** (gâteau→gâteaux, chapeau→chapeaux, oiseau→oiseaux, jeu→jeux). Distractors = the SINGULAR + the OVER-APPLIED-OTHER-RULE (chat→*chatx / gâteau→*gâteaus) so the -s-vs-x choice is load-bearing (mirrors the core's `wrongPlural` design). (a) **5 arms** → fr: speak `'fr-FR'` · sayable `'plusieurs'` (___-placeholder) · setupTask `(de||fr)&&round.chips` · isCorrect `(de||fr)&&round.answer` · duckSVG aria « Marguerite la cane » (⚠ the aria was a HARDCODED `'Daisy the duck'` de left un-localized — added a fr branch) ; (b) **7 `strings`** → fr all (title « Marguerite la cane », 🦆 kept). **⚠ MASCOT — DESCRIPTIVE → TRANSLATE-TO-A-MEANING-CARRYING-NATIVE-NAME: Daisy = the daisy FLOWER = « la marguerite » in French, AND « Marguerite » is a real French girl's name → « Marguerite »** (de renamed to a fresh « Ella » ; fr's « Marguerite » carries the flower-meaning + is native). 57. **atlas-fact-files `key-detail.ri-k-1`** → « Les fiches d'Atlas — Lire un texte documentaire (CP) » / slug `repondre-a-une-question-texte-documentaire-cp` — commit `4d0dfb85`. **CP**, RI.K.1, **répondre à une question sur un texte documentaire / prélever une information explicite** — **21st Group-C build = THE LAST surveyed Group-C EN-only activity** (no fork; only one remained). Atlas le tatou montre un court texte documentaire de 2 phrases (affiché + 🔊), pose une question en Qu-, l'enfant tape la réponse parmi 3 (la réponse est VERBATIM dans le texte). **The activity ALREADY had a de fan-out** (Klasse 1) → additive fr content. **`fact-detail-core.js`/`lcs-shell.*`/`strand-names.ts` = 0 lines.** **⚡ NO HARD GATE:** answer = `option===answer`-derived (`oracle=options.indexOf(answer)`), core `facts.answerInFact` = `norm(fact).indexOf(norm(answer))>=0`; `verify-fact-detail-core.js` reads only en `params.rounds` (no `--locale`) → PASS untouched; fr bank validated STRUCTURALLY with the REAL core. **8 rounds** (bees/spider/cow/frog/penguin/sun/tree/rain), all TRUE facts, « manchot » (not pingouin) for the ice bird. **Rulings (native ensemble):** (1) **GRADE = CP → fr GRADE_OVERRIDE '1'** — native 'K' does NOT auto-map to GS here: the crux is the MODALITÉ (lu-vs-entendu, not difficulty). The fact is DISPLAYED as readable text (2 phrases) → « comprendre un texte LU par soi-même » = CP « Lecture et compréhension de l'écrit »; le 🔊 = étayage; « prélever une info explicite dans un court texte » = attendu canonique CP; the 3 options are written statements the child READS + matches. German Klasse 1 corroborates. **Contrast nila-pond #47 (RI.K.2) = GS** — that one is main-idea ENTENDU (pré-lecteur listens) → GS; atlas is literal prélèvement in a READ text → CP. The GS/CP split is lu-vs-entendu, NOT facile-vs-difficile. **⚠ Added `fr:'1'` to the EXISTING atlas GRADE_OVERRIDE entry `{de:'1'}`.** (2) **STRAND = fr STRAND_OVERRIDE « Lecture et compréhension de l'écrit »** (= #50 bea, U+2019 « l'écrit ») — compréhension LITTÉRALE d'un texte lu; NOT « Comprendre et interpréter » (sur-revendique l'inférence — ici récupération verbatim, aucune interprétation); NOT « Écouter de l'écrit et comprendre » (label GS/pré-lecteur — willow/nila/story-spine). **NEW STRAND entry.** (3) **⚠ SOUNDNESS + DISTRACTOR DESIGN CORRECTION (the reconciliation):** the linguist's distractors were all HORS-TEXTE, which lets a child solve by VISUAL WORD-MATCHING (« which option has words from the text? ») WITHOUT reading the question. The pedagogue's recipe (ADOPTED — I revised all 8 rounds' distractors): 3 options = **(1) verbatim answer / (2) a distractor that IS in the text but answers a DIFFERENT Qu- question** (« du miel doré » for « Où… ? » → forces linking Qu-word→right info) **/ (3) a plausible same-category hors-texte distractor.** Qu- types où/que/combien OK; quand only with an explicit temporal marker (« pendant le jour » ✓); AVOID pourquoi (inference). Facts TRUE + verified (spider = « huit pattes », arachnid, not called an insect). (4) « Atlas le tatou » kept (tatou=armadillo; « Atlas »=atlas/savoir documentaire — thematically ideal). **⚠ ENCODING (= #52…#56):** activity JS `strings.fr` + manifest `roundsL10n.fr` = **U+2019** — the answer is matched by exact string AND is a substring of the fact → the fact AND the answer/in-text-distractor MUST use the SAME U+2019 apostrophe so the substring holds (« de l'herbe verte »/« de l'ombre fraîche »); 53. **gabby-sayings `idioms.l-3-5-a`** → « Les drôles d'expressions de Gabby — Le sens figuré (CE2) » / slug `comprendre-les-expressions-imagees-sens-figure-ce2` — commit `5a2ee07c`. **CE2**, L.3.5.a, **les expressions imagées / le sens figuré (sens propre vs sens figuré)** — 17th Group-C build, operator-chosen from a 4-way fork (idiomes / noms-collectifs / vocab-en-situation / portrait) for variety (reading-comp was 7 builds) + fun/culturally-distinctive. Gabby l'oie montre une expression imagée dans une phrase (lue + 🔊); l'enfant tape le SENS FIGURÉ (réponse) vs un piège LITTÉRAL (reprend les noms concrets de l'expression) vs une carte OFF (sans rapport). **The activity ALREADY had a de fan-out** (native German sayings — Daumen drücken/Schwein haben) → additive fr content. **`idiom-meaning-core.js`/`lcs-shell.*`/`strand-names.ts` = 0 lines.** **⚡ NO HARD GATE:** the answer is `kind==='correct'`-derived (childView hides `kind`); `verify-idiom-meaning-core.js` reads only the en `params.rounds` (no `--locale`) → PASS untouched; the fr bank validated STRUCTURALLY with the REAL core. **9 native French idiom rounds** (chat-gorge, pleut-cordes, chair-poule, faim-loup, tomber-pommes, dans-la-lune, memoire-elephant, poil-main, langue-chat). **⚠ FIGURATIVE LANGUAGE IS RE-AUTHORED PER LOCALE, NOT TRANSLATED** (native French idioms, like the de bank's native German ones — an English-idiom translation would be nonsense). **⚠ THE LOAD-BEARING CONTENT CONSTRAINT: the `literal` card must out-overlap the `correct` card on the IDIOM's words** (`overlapCount(choice, idiom)` in the core = shared non-stopword tokens; the literal echoes the idiom's concrete nouns → highest overlap → the "pick the card most like the saying" shortcut grabs the trap + MISSES). Verified with the REAL core: on all 9 rounds the LITERAL is the strict max-overlap (correct NEVER is; `maxOverlapIndex !== correctIndex`), overlapBot=0. **⚠ The core's STOP list is ENGLISH** (under-filters French function words le/la/un/de…) but the property holds because the correct card avoids the idiom's CONCRETE NOUNS entirely (the linguist even dropped « pleut » from the pleut-cordes correct card so it wouldn't out-overlap); concrete nouns are apostrophe-free (chat/gorge/cordes/pommes/loup/poule/lune/poil/main/langue/éléphant) → clean overlap. **Rulings (native ensemble):** (1) **GRADE = CE2 — NO GRADE_OVERRIDE** (native '3' auto-maps to CE2): sens propre/sens figuré + expressions imagées = l'attendu CE2 « le lexique » (« identifier l'utilisation d'un mot/expression au sens propre et au sens figuré »); CE1 too early (familles/synonymes/antonymes), CM1 = approfondissement not introduction. **3rd fr activity needing NO grade override** (after #50 grade1→CP, #51 grade2→CE1). (2) **STRAND = fr STRAND_OVERRIDE « Le lexique »** — le sens figuré = travail sur le sens des mots/expressions = « le lexique » (= antonyms/nuances/categories #27/#34/#35/#46); NOT « Comprendre et interpréter » (that's reading-comprehension of a text), NOT default « Étude de la langue ». **⚠ Added `fr:'Le lexique'` to the EXISTING gabby STRAND_OVERRIDE entry `{de:'Wortschatz untersuchen'}`** (didn't create a duplicate row). Core `fix-it-core.js` fully locale-neutral + **index-driven** → 0 core lines. **THE INDEX SEMANTICS (verified against the real `applyRepair`):** capitalize `t[targetIndex]=cap(...)` (in place; replacement decorative) · insert-punct/insert-word `t.splice(gapIndex,0,replacement)` (insert AT gapIndex; append=tokens.length) · swap `t[targetIndex]=replacement` (chooser=[replacement,...distractors], broken NOT re-offered) · reorder `clean[i]=tokens[correctOrder[i]]` (source indices, no period token) · delete `t.splice(targetIndex,1)` · split `t.splice(seamIndex+1,0,'.')` then `cap(t[seamIndex+2])` (2nd-start LOWERCASE in tokens → engine caps). **Rulings (native French sentence-repair ensemble; indices computed + build-asserted `applyRepair(round)===clean` for all 7):** (1) **7 rounds, each a UNIQUE fix:** cap « le chien est grand. »→Le (French caps ONLY the first word — no German noun-capital trap) · punct « Le soleil est chaud[.] » distractors [,?] · swap « Le chat mangent→mange » distractors [manges,mangeons] (**broken « mangent » excluded from the chooser, per German**; « Le chat » unambiguously 3sg) · insert-word « La fille [mange] une pomme. » distractors [jolie(adj),avec(prép)] (article « une » already present → only a verb completes it, NO partitive needed) · reorder « vite court chien Le »→« Le chien court vite » (capital-first + S-V-Adv unique, no period) · delete « Ma maman [elle] cuisine. » (colloquial subject-DOUBLING → delete « elle », the classic written-French correction) · split « Je cours[.] Il fait beau ». **No apostrophe-elision in any subject** (Le/La/Ma — tokens are separate). (2) **GRADE = CE1 (manifest "2", NO GRADE_OVERRIDE)** — réviser une phrase / accord sujet-verbe / ordre des mots = CE1 attendus; matches German Klasse 2 one-for-one. (3) **Strand « Étude de la langue »** — already set, **NO strand-names change**. (4) **« Dr. Plume »** kept (un hibou); title **« La clinique des phrases du Dr. Plume »**; functional kid framing (« le verbe va avec le sujet »); « l'accord sujet-verbe » parent-prose only. **Tech (activity-layer only):** German build had var LANG + `strings`(14 keys: title/instruction/promptCap-Punct-Swap-Insert-Reorder-Delete-Split/alright/giggle/soundsRight/hintCheck, resolved via `api.t()`+`PROMPT_FOR`) + `_buildTasksFromRow` (AUTO-uses `roundsL10n[LANG]` → 0 JS for round data) + a binary `LANG==='de'?'de-DE':'en-US'` in `speak()`'s SpeechSynthesis fallback; French = `strings.fr`(14) + the `speak()` fr-FR branch + `roundsL10n.fr`(7, each `{id,band,action,tokens,<action-index-fields>,convention,clean}`; convention localized). 24. **pronoun `case.l-1-1-d`** → « La boutique de Hattie » / slug `les-pronoms-choisir-la-bonne-forme-ce2` — commit `de1154f3`. **CE2**, L.1.1.d, **les pronoms (sujet/objet/possessif)** — 5th French literacy. Hattie le lièvre's hat shop; the child picks the right pronoun form from **2 chips** for a sentence blank. 9 rounds = 3 subject + 3 object + 3 possessive; French pronouns by FUNCTION — sujet (Il/Je/Ils) / COD (me/te/le) / déterminant possessif (mon/ton/notre); the same person recurs across roles (1st Je/me/mon · 3rd Il/le · plural Ils/notre → "same person, different form by its job"). **ENGINEERING CRUX:** `pronoun-core.js` hardcodes an ENGLISH `CASE_TABLE` (i/he/they/we) — `chipStrings`/`isAnswer` derive purely from it. German carried its own `de:{correct,wrong}` per round + activity-layer wrappers `pnChips`/`pnIsAnswer` branching `(LANG==='de'&&r.de)`. **French did the same via `fr:{correct,wrong}` — I GENERALIZED the wrapper guard from `LANG==='de'&&r.de` to `r[LANG]`** (`var loc=r&&r[LANG]; return (loc&&loc.correct)?[loc.correct,loc.wrong]:Core.chipStrings(r)`) → behavior-identical for en [no `r.en`→Core] + de [`r.de`→forms], adds fr; **0 core lines**. Proven byte-identical by a functional node regression (en==Core, de==de.forms, fr grades only fr.correct). **Rulings (native French pronoun ensemble):** (1) **9 rounds, every wrong chip provably ungrammatical:** subject « ___ court vite. » Il/Le · « ___ mange une pomme. » Je/Me · « ___ chantent ensemble. » Ils/Les; object « Maman ___ voit. » me/je · « Papa ___ porte. » te/tu · « Léo ___ suit. » le/son; possessive « C'est ___ ballon. » mon/me · « Voici ___ chien. » ton/te · « C'est ___ classe. » notre/nous. (2) **THREE French traps, all navigated:** **T1 elision** — every subject/object verb consonant-initial (court/mange/chantent/voit/porte/suit) → me/te/le stay whole (no m'/t'/l'); **T2 subject-doubling** — object rounds' wrong subject-pronoun blocked by verb agreement (« Maman je voit » fails: voit≠vois; same tu/porte), O3's wrong « son » is a possessive → dislocation structurally impossible; **T3 article-ambiguity** — possessive wrong chips are me/te/nous (NEVER the article-homograph le/la/les → « C'est me ballon » unambiguously wrong). No answer-leak (asserted). (3) **GRADE = CE2 → fr GRADE_OVERRIDE '3'** (beside de:'3'): les pronoms sujets = CE1, but the **COD/object pronoun** (me/te/le) — the activity's central difficulty — is a CE2 attendu → honest floor CE2. Mirrors German Klasse 3. (4) **Strand « Étude de la langue »** (pronoms/classes de mots) — already set, **NO strand-names change**. (5) Functional kid framing (« qui FAIT l'action ? »/« de qui parle la phrase ? »/« c'est à qui ? » — NO pronom/COD jargon in bubbles); **« Hattie »** kept (un lièvre); title **« La boutique de Hattie »**. **Tech (activity-layer only):** German build had var LANG + txt() + `pnChips`/`pnIsAnswer` (all 4 chip/answer call-sites go through them) + L(9 keys: q/win/hear/noteSubject/noteObject/notePossessive/nSubject/nObject/nPossessive, picked by `round.role`) + strings(3) + `roundsL10n[LANG]` + THREE binary `LANG==='de'?…:en` inline ternaries (hear blank-word `Lücke`, `_srMirror` separator ` oder `, `_srMirror` blank-word); French = the `r[LANG]` wrapper generalization + `L.fr`(9) + `strings.fr`(3) + fr branches in the 3 ternaries (`trou`/` ou `/`trou`) + `roundsL10n.fr`(9, each `{id, role, sentence, fr:{correct, wrong}}` — subject forms capitalized sentence-initial, object/possessive lowercase). Core 0 lines. 54/54 fr + 54/54 EN-regression first pass; personal Read confirmed all 3 role types render (Il/Le, me/je, mon/me), correct capitalization, Hattie the hare + little hat, « Écouter », C'est apostrophe clean.
